# The inventory: every judged bar line of rounds 27–34, with its noise (sizing S1)

Generated by `python3 tools/judge/inventory.py` from `tools/judge/lines.py` (the lines as the streams quoted them in `_agents/workstreams.md` *Bars* and *Rulings* and the tables under `references/round27..34/`) and the rows kept there; the csv beside this file (`inventory.csv`) is the full table, this page is its reading. Every number is the streams' own (builder0 unless the line says otherwise, the commit in its column); the noise columns are computed here from the rows.

**Columns.** *noise se*: the se of the line's statistic (a paired sum, mean or bootstrap median difference over the (seed, tick) pairs; the table's own se where no rows were kept; a binomial or Poisson sd for counts). *line x*: the line's margin from "no change" in the statistic's units (the guard's X, 25 % of the base, 2 se). **Line inside the noise = Y** when x < 1.645 se: a treatment that changes nothing fails that guard (or passes that gain line) more than 5 % of the time at that n, so the line could not be judged at that n whatever the result. *verdict margin (se)*: how many se the observed value cleared (+) or missed (−) the line by; |margin| < 1.645 means another seed set could have flipped the verdict. *exact p*: Fisher / McNemar for count lines. *n decides*: the n per arm at which a count line of the observed rates reaches 80 % power (Fisher, one-sided 5 %).

## The count

| | lines |
|---|---|
| judged lines listed (PASS / FAIL / KEPT) | 575 |
| lines with a noise estimate (rows or a printed se) | 302 |
| **judged pass lines INSIDE the noise** (x < 1.645 se; 14 of them pure direction checks with x = 0) | **61** |
| judged verdicts within 1.645 se of their line (could flip on another seed set) | 142 |
| lines where the arms were byte-identical (equality checks, 0 noise) | 15 |
| lines with no noise estimate possible (deterministic cells, reported, no rows) | 283 |
